# IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN.md — NexusOps

> **Phase note.** Spec-anchored task tracker for the NexusOps MVP, decomposed from the binding `ARCHITECTURE.md` (every phase cites its `§N` anchors; cross-doc-invariant models mirror `ARCHITECTURE.md` Appendix A). Scope is the **comprehensive MVP** the owner locked at arch-finalize (`§0.1`): both Claude Code **and** Codex live (O-1), **full resume-or-replay** survival (O-2), **full multi-step Brain action plans** (O-3), Claude supervision mode pending-spike (O-4), `NexusOps-ui-kit`/"Graphite Arc" as the canonical design system (O-5), full **PR Review Workspace** (O-6). macOS-only. **Built to the architecture contract; acceptance = the E2E final-smoke matrix** (`§19.1`; `docs/runbooks/e2e-acceptance-matrix.md` — accreted per slice, NOT a single PRD §25 demo path; user-decided 2026-06-14). Build order = invariants → lifecycle correctness → tests → E2E smoke → polish. Living-state sections below start empty and accrete through `/tdd` work.
>
> **Re-derived production-grade (2026-06-11, posture migration):** decomposition re-anchored to the production-grade `ARCHITECTURE.md` (Build-posture line + the new `§2.5` dependency DAG). New machinery: the §2.5-derived **Parallelization plan (Track map)** with critical path + forced-serial bottleneck; per-task **`Depends on:`** edges (contract flow, not habit-order); **one discrete §18 benchmark task per budgeted hot path** (own cadence — never in the per-slice RED/GREEN loop); **covered-by waivers** on completed phases' Spec anchors lines (existing pre-tag tests are honored; only NEW tests carry `spec(§X)` tags); production concerns (CI gates, error paths, idempotency, audit baselines) promoted early. **Completion state reconciled in place** — Phase 0/1 + 2.0-SEC + Phase-6 ticks, evidence, and all living sections preserved, not reset.
>
> **Reading discipline.** Read this file **by section, not whole** — `/orchestrate-start` and `/session-start` grep the section header and read only "Currently in progress" + the active phase. The living sections below (Currently-in-progress, Carry-forward, Log, Trims, Decisions) are **bounded** — pruned/archived at `/orchestrate-end`, never left to grow — so a sectioned read stays cheap even late in the project.

> **Session protocol:**
> - **At session start** — orchestrator runs `/orchestrate-start`; implementer runs `/session-start`. Confirm what's targeted this session. **Re-read the phase's `Spec anchors:` sections of `ARCHITECTURE.md` first.**
> - **At session end** (only when the user says we're done):
>   - **Implementer** runs `/session-end` — TDD audit + cross-doc audit + Step-9 list + session doc + `/preflight`. Does NOT touch this doc.
>   - **Orchestrator** runs `/orchestrate-end` — verify hot routing landed, reconcile checkbox state, append Log entry, update Decisions / Carry-forward / Currently in progress, triage Carry-forward, round commit + push.

> **Reference deadlines:**
> - Solo + AI build (cc-crew); no hard calendar deadline — optimize for the PRD §25 demo as the proof.
> - **Phase 0 spikes gate their dependent phases** — do not start a phase whose gating spike is unresolved (see Phase 0).

> **Spec-anchor convention (architecture-as-contract).** Each phase header carries a `**Spec anchors:**` block listing the `ARCHITECTURE.md` sections it implements. Orchestrator + implementer re-read those anchors at session start. If a slice surfaces a behavior the anchors don't cover, that's a cross-doc invariant flag at TDD Step 9 — either the anchor is missing (→ re-run `/arch-finalize`) or the implementation drifted. Architecture is contract; drift surfaces structurally.

## Phase exit checklist (template — applies to every phase)

Before ticking a phase complete (**executed row-by-row by `/phase-exit <phase>`** — the orchestrator
dispatches it at the START of a round; each row is ticked in place as it passes):

- [ ] **All phase task checkboxes ticked.** Conservative — partial work stays unchecked with a Log note.
- [ ] **Acceptance criterion met.** `/preflight` clean + manual smoke if there's runtime behavior.
- [ ] **`/preflight` clean.** Includes architecture-invariant tests (esp. INV-SEC-1, §15).
- [ ] **Cross-doc invariants verified.** No model field change without an `ARCHITECTURE.md` Appendix A edit in the same round.
- [ ] **Reachability audit clean per touched area** (`reachability-auditor`).
- [ ] **Arch-drift audit clean over the phase's Spec anchors** (`arch-drift-auditor`).
- [ ] **Spec coverage: every phase anchor has a tagged test or waiver** (`scripts/spec-lint.sh tests <phase>`).

<!-- Posture-gated rows (production-grade — recorded in the manifest at the M-0011 upgrade, 2026-06-11): -->
- [ ] **Dependency audit: no NEW findings vs the accepted-risk baseline** — `/phase-exit` runs `cargo audit && (cd ui && pnpm audit --prod)` once; one-line new-vs-baseline summary (full output → `docs/audits/`); a new finding is accepted-risk-recorded in Decisions tabled or escalated as a **Finding**. _(production-grade)_
- [ ] **Whole-system security review clean (qualifying phases).** Qualifies when the phase carries security-/invariant-tagged tasks or trust-boundary surfaces (per `docs/planning/THREAT_MODEL.md` + the §15 anchors). Executor resolves from the reviewer policy (`invariant`): the default tool is the built-in `/security-review` over the branch's pending changes (the track's accumulated diff), with gstack `/cso` as the heavier escalation when trust-boundary anchors are in scope. Critical findings escalate as **Findings**. _(production-grade)_
- [ ] **Perf budgets met, or the regression is flagged as a Finding** — run the phase's benchmark task(s) against the `ARCHITECTURE.md §18` budgets; phases with no stated budgets tick with `n/a — no budgets (deliberate deferral recorded)`. _(production-grade, when budgets exist)_

- [ ] **Session doc(s) for this phase exist** and list every file created/modified.
- [ ] **Commits pushed to the git remote.**

## Final-submission acceptance criteria (project-level)

The MVP is "done" when:

- [ ] The **E2E final-smoke acceptance matrix** passes end-to-end locally (`§19.1`; `docs/runbooks/e2e-acceptance-matrix.md` — the accreted release gate; the PRD §25 path is one flow within it).
- [ ] **INV-SEC-1** holds and is tested: no mutation path bypasses the Action Gateway (`§15`).
- [ ] Both **Claude Code and Codex** sessions launch, are supervised with reliable non-scraped status, and survive UI restart + resume-or-replay across daemon restart (`§9.1`, `§17`, O-1/O-2).
- [ ] The app is **signed + notarized** (incl. the Python Brain sidecar) and first-run bootstrap + consent map work (`§16`).
- [ ] Accessibility MUSTs pass: graph list/table fallback, focus ring, drag→non-drag (`§11.6`, tested `§14`).

## Parallelization plan (Track map)

> **Authority chain:** `ARCHITECTURE.md §2.5` (subsystem DAG + seams) → this Track map (plan-level rendering, refined by per-task `Depends on:` edges) → `/team-start <track>` (consumes the table below). Track names here are the ONLY valid `<track>` prefixes (root `CLAUDE.md` "Naming + cross-bleed prevention"). Re-derived 2026-06-11 at the production-posture migration; supersedes the 2026-06-07 "Parallelization & track plan" (whose 2-track shape was validated in practice: daemon Phase 0–1 ∥ ui Phase 6 both completed on it).

**Phase/track DAG** (nodes = phases; edges = `Depends on (phases)`; ✅ = done):

```
  P0 shared freeze ✅ ──┬──────────────────────────────────────────────┐
                        ▼                                              ▼
  P1 daemon spine ✅ ──► P2 GATEWAY ◄─────────────── (mockable iface)  P6 ui shell ✅ (modulo parked tail)
        (CONTRACT 0.14.0)   │ the forced-serial bottleneck                  │
                            ├────────────► P5 git/profiles (edges, vs frozen iface)
                            ├────────────► P7.1 GitHub/Linear daemon side (edges)
                            ├────────────► P6-tail 6.3d/e + intent seam (ui; + P3.4 for the Terminal well)
                            ▼
                        P3 harness + terminal (claude ∥ codex ∥ FakePty intra-phase)
                            ▼
                        P4 survival/failure-contract
                            │           P8 Brain seam (converge; ◄ P2 + 0.2-HITL)
                            │           P9 workflow packs (converge; ◄ P1+P2; Plan view ◄ P6)
                            ▼
                        P10 packaging + signing + PRD §25 demo  (integration — ALL-HANDS, ◄ everything incl. 0.2)
```

**Critical path (serial floor):** **P2 → P3 → P4 → P10.** **Forced-serial bottleneck: Phase 2 (the Gateway)** — every mutating executor (P5/P7), the harness intercept (P3), the Brain proposal path (P8), and the ui mutation/intent seam (parked 6.3d/e + Gateway modal) all queue behind the P2 contract freeze (mutation methods + `ActionRequest`/`ActionPlan` + the §6.3 catalog). This mirrors the 0.5 contract freeze one layer up: **freeze the action contract early in P2, then fan out.**

**Track map** (`/team-start <track>` reads this; team naming `<track>-<area>-<role>`):

| Track | Phases | Area(s) | Worktree | Team name | Gated by | Status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| **daemon** (critical path) | 2 → 3 → 4 | `daemon/`, `shared/` | main checkout | `nexusops-daemon` (`daemon-orchestrator` / `daemon-implementer`) | — (unblocked) | **ACTIVE-NEXT** (2.1) |
| **ui** | 6-tail (6.3d/e + intent seam) → 7.2/7.3-UI → 8.2-UI | `ui/` | `track/ui` worktree | `nexusops-ui` (`ui-ui-orchestrator` / `ui-ui-implementer`) | P2.1 mutation-contract freeze (+ P3.4 Terminal Channel for 6.3d's well) | PAUSED (resume at P2 freeze) |
| **edges** (optional 3rd) | 5 ∥ 7.1 | `daemon/` | `track/edges` worktree | `nexusops-edges` (`edges-daemon-orchestrator` / `edges-daemon-implementer`) | P2 Gateway-iface freeze (S4 — mock to start), 0.3 | OPTIONAL (bandwidth) |
| _(converge — not a parallel track)_ | 8, 9 | `daemon/`+`ui/` | main | _(daemon team absorbs)_ | P2 (+0.2 HITL for P8 bundling) | queued |
| _(integration — final gate)_ | 10 | all | main | all-hands | ~everything (esp. 0.2 notarization) | queued |

**Integration/merge order (multi-track):** edges → main at each P5/P7.1 phase-exit (daemon-area code, same crate — rebase-merge, run `/phase-exit`); ui → main at its round seals (established pattern: `46ed874`). The daemon track owns `shared/` — **only the daemon track bumps `CONTRACT_VERSION`**; ui/edges consume via regen (the §5.0 mechanism).

**Shared contracts across tracks** (freeze before fan-out — the §2.5-seam model list): the P2 Gateway surface (`ActionRequest`/`ActionPlan`/`Approval`/`ActionResult` §6.2 + `ActionTypeCatalog` §6.3 + the `submit_*`/`approve`/`deny`/`preview` wire methods §6.1/§6.4) is the next freeze; EventEnvelope/IDs/status machines/EventTypeRegistry (§7.1/§5.1/§5.2) already frozen @ 0.14.0; `HarnessAdapter` normalized types (§9.1) freeze at P3.1; `BrainEventMapping` (§13.1) at P8.1.

**Ceiling (unchanged guidance, validated):** 2 tracks is the sweet spot, 3 with bandwidth — the human is the escalation conduit for every team and becomes the bottleneck past ~3. **Don't fan a track out before its gating contract freezes** — contract churn after fan-out thrashes every consumer (the 0.5 lesson, now applied to the P2 action contract).

## Phase 1 — Daemon foundation: event store, projections, IPC, locks, bootstrap

**Goal:** The trust-core spine — a detached Rust daemon owning a single-writer WAL SQLite event store with rebuildable projections, an outbox, persistent lease locks, a UDS `GatewayPort`, single-instance + first-run bootstrap, and migrations with backup/rollback. Everything downstream writes through this.

**Spec anchors:** `ARCHITECTURE.md §4`, `§6.1`, `§6.4`, `§7`, `§7.1`, `§7.2`, `§5.2`, `§8` (recovery rows), `§16` (bootstrap/migration/version-compat), `§12`. **Waivers (phase ✅ pre-tagging — existing untagged suites honored; new tests carry `spec(§X)`):** §4 (covered-by: daemon/tests/runtime.rs) · §6.1 (covered-by: daemon/tests/ipc.rs) · §6.4 (covered-by: daemon/tests/ipc.rs) · §7 (covered-by: daemon/tests/eventstore.rs + projections.rs + outbox.rs) · §7.1 (covered-by: shared/tests/envelope.rs) · §7.2 (covered-by: daemon/tests/replay.rs) · §5.2 (covered-by: shared/tests/contract.rs) · §8 (covered-by: daemon/tests/replay.rs recovery suite) · §16 (covered-by: daemon/tests/eventstore.rs + runtime.rs bootstrap suites) · §12 (covered-by: daemon/tests/runtime.rs write-actor suite).

**Track:** `daemon` (critical path) · **Depends on (phases):** 0 (0.4, 0.5). _(✅ DONE 2026-06-10, seal `310df20`.)_

- [x] `leases` table (one row per **`(resource_id, lease_kind)`**: owner_id, fencing_token, acquired_at, heartbeat_at, expires_at — Appendix A field set; MVP_TASKS abbreviation reconciled); owner-guarded renew (token unchanged); expired-lease reclaim mints a new token; `pidlock` single-instance; `reap_once` reaper unit. — **`fencing_token` = persisted monotonic high-water mark (+1 on acquire/reclaim under `BEGIN IMMEDIATE`; release/reap KEEP it; survives restart). ## Phase 2 — Action Gateway (the mutation chokepoint)

**Goal:** The single, audited mutator. Typed `ActionRequest`/`ActionPlan` over the GatewayPort, the staged pipeline (normalize → resolve → policy/risk → preview/dry-run → approval → execute → audit), risk 0-4, **bundled ActionPlan + step-by-step approval (O-3)**, idempotency, fencing-guarded execution, stale-precondition re-check, fail-closed-on-audit-write, and the MVP executor adapters' interface.

**Spec anchors:** `ARCHITECTURE.md §6`, `§6.1`, `§6.2`, `§6.3`, `§5.1` (Approval/ActionRequest), `§5.2` (covered-by: shared/tests/contract.rs — the action/approval/plan IDs the Gateway contract introduces), `§7.1` (covered-by: shared/tests + daemon golden-log — the `ActionExecution*` EventTypeRegistry entries the Gateway emits), `§7.2` (source-of-truth / re-read invariant — the executor read-source [2.3] + stale-precondition re-read [2.4]; "rows canonical for execution"), `§15` (INV-SEC-1, fail-closed), `§17` (stale-precondition, fencing-conflict, daemon-crash-mid-action), `§2.5` (non-TDD: planning DAG — realized by the Track map + per-task Depends-on edges, not testable code; the Gateway = forced-serial bottleneck, freeze the action contract early then fan out), plus for the promoted ops tasks: §5.0 (non-TDD: the CI schema-diff/3-way gates ARE the verification) · §14 (non-TDD: CI pipeline config; the §14 tiers run in CI) · §18 (benchmark) · §16 (non-TDD: the dependency-audit baseline — `cargo audit`/`pnpm audit --prod` CI gate — part of the §16 supply-chain posture; promoted-ops task 2.6).

**Track:** `daemon` (critical path — THE bottleneck phase, §2.5) · **Depends on (phases):** 1 (✅), 0 (✅) — **UNBLOCKED.** Unblocks at its contract freeze: `edges` (P5/P7.1 vs the frozen Gateway iface), the ui mutation/intent seam (parked 6.3d/e), P3 intercept→intent, P8 Brain proposals.

## Phase 3 — Harness adapters & embedded terminal

**Goal:** One `HarnessAdapter` contract over two lifecycle models — Claude Code (drive-mode per the 0.1 spike; `can_use_tool`/`PreToolUse` defense-in-depth, default-mode-only, no background subagents) and Codex (`app-server` stdio). Embedded PTY terminals (portable-pty → xterm.js) with backpressure. Status derived from structured streams, never from PTY scraping.

**Spec anchors:** `ARCHITECTURE.md §9.1`, `§5.1` (Session machine), `§6` (intercept→Gateway intent), `§7.2` (harness-derived SoT), `§9` (terminal/ADR-009), `§0.1` O-1/O-4, `§0.2` O-13, `§18 (benchmark)`.

**Track:** `daemon` (critical path — P4 survival depends on adapters existing; re-assigned from `edges` at the 2026-06-11 re-derivation per §2.5) · **Depends on (phases):** 2 (intercept→intent iface; mockable to start early), 0 (0.1 Claude-mode ruling ← HITL drain; 0.3 Codex schema capture ← HITL). **Intra-phase parallel (S3):** Claude adapter ∥ Codex adapter ∥ FakeHarness/FakePty behind the one trait; terminal (3.4) parallel to status derivation (S6).

## Phase 4 — Session lifecycle, survival & failure-mode contract

**Goal:** Full session survival (O-2) and the consolidated failure-mode contract: UI-restart reconnect-live; daemon-restart resume-or-replay (`claude --resume`/`codex thread/resume` else scrollback replay + relaunch); supervised-child-death recovery; the background jobs; the §17 failure table behaviors and their UI-feeding events.

**Spec anchors:** `ARCHITECTURE.md §17`, `§10`, `§8` (recovery rows **+ the B2-strict detachable-terminal/broker survival EXTENSION** — user-ruled 2026-06-12, deep-dive §8.1, → next `/arch-finalize`), `§0.1` O-2, `§5.1` (stale, Session terminals), `§9.1` (the live `HarnessAdapter` drive loop), `§6.2`/`§6.3` (the Gateway session-create + the agent-mutation catalog, live), `§15` (INV-SEC-1 — the live interception, cat-1).

> **P4 forks RULED 2026-06-12 (deep-dive §8 = the finalized slice order):** (b) **B2-strict** live-reattach (the broker subsystem is in P4 scope, a user-ruled §8 EXTENSION) · (a) **opt-3** session-as-supervised-actor · (c) freeze `ResumeMode = resumed|replayed|relaunched|reattached_live` at the head of 4.1 · d.1 §6.2 approval-wait knob (default ~5 min, fail-closed LOCKED) · d.2 split tool-policy · d.3 MCP/Task/bg-subagent denied (MVP). **The cat-1 boundary:** launching a session is a Gateway mutation + a live agent is unsafe un-intercepted → the live spawn + the live interception land TOGETHER as the cat-1 **4.0b**; everything before is FakeHarness scaffolding.

**Track:** `daemon` (critical path) · **Depends on (phases):** 1 (✅), 3 (sessions/adapters must exist to survive/recover). **Parallel-with:** edges (P5/P7.1), ui-tail.

### 4.0b-2-F2 — separate the intercept-wait permit class from the UI-approval permit pool — ✅ **DONE 2026-06-13** (`4a7572a`; brief 055 [spec-lint `@6ada5f8c`]; 392→398 [+6 tests]; **security-reviewer CLEAR, 0 findings**; code-quality 1 medium + 3 low fixed-in-slice; **reserved sub-bound** `wait_cap=48=MAX−RESERVED(16)`; fail-closed-on-exhaustion, no-bypass; PURE `intercept_verdict_with_wait_class` seam + **/wired LOAD-BEARING confirmed** [wait_cap=48 in production]; **NO CONTRACT bump** [daemon-internal accept-loop/IPC]; LESSON §34) (NEAR-TERM production hardening — lead-ruled 2026-06-13; availability/INV-SEC-1-adjacent; implements §6.4/§10; origin: 2026-06-13 4.0b-2 C2 F2)
- [x] **Finding (4.0b-2 C2 security pass, F2) — RESOLVED:** the live `intercept` waits + the UI `approve`/`deny` connections share the **single 64-permit (`MAX_CONNECTIONS`) accept pool** → a flood of concurrent mutating intercept-waits (each holding a permit up to the 5-min approval timeout) can **exhaust the pool and block the UI from approving** → fail-closed denials = **degraded availability**. **Production posture (lead):** the user's multi-Max-plan goal = REAL concurrent multi-agent supervision → this is a genuine production concern, **near-term hardening (not someday-maybe)**. Fail-SAFE (toward Deny), not a bypass.
- [x] **Fix (AS-BUILT): the reserved sub-bound** (Step-2.5 Option A) — a 2nd `Semaphore(wait_cap = MAX − RESERVED = 48 of 64)`; a parked wait holds it alongside its general permit → `wait_cap<MAX` guarantees `RESERVED=16` general slots ALWAYS free for the UI/reads (no accept-time classification, no mid-connection release). Wait-class exhaustion → fail-closed Deny (`INTERCEPT_SATURATED_REASON`, distinct from timeout; no park, **no bypass** — fail-SAFE; acquire AFTER the audit commit, before the wait). PURE `intercept_verdict_with_wait_class` seam + `InterceptWaitClass` wrapper; **security-reviewer CLEAR** (no-starvation + fail-closed-on-exhaustion + no-bypass + no-leak + the live wiring).

## Phase 6 — Frontend shell & projection-driven UI

**Goal:** The Tauri shell as a projection-driven reattaching client implementing the canonical design system (O-5), the status-rendering binding, attention-first ordering, the core screens, the net-new daemon/survival/degraded surfaces, and the accessibility MUSTs.

**Spec anchors:** `ARCHITECTURE.md §11`, `§11.1`–`§11.7`, `§4` (UI=client), `§5.1` (status binding), `§7.2` (degraded SoT), `§14` (frontend tests), `§18 (benchmark)`, `§6.1` (GatewayPort — the ui transport client), `§6.4` (IPC wire contract — the codec/handshake/frames), `§5.0` (contract types the client (de)serializes). _(§6.1/§6.4/§5.0 added 2026-06-13 — the live `UdsGatewayPort` transport [task 6.8] consumes them; covered-by: the transport slices 049+ [`ui/gateway-uds` + `uds.ts`], in progress.)_ **Waivers (Phase-6 logic+visual ✅ pre-tagging; parked 6.3d/e surfaces covered at their unpark slices):** §11 (covered-by: ui/src 43 *.test.tsx suites, 214 cases) · §11.1 (covered-by: ui/src/theme tests + visual gate) · §11.2 (covered-by: ui/src/views + shell tests) · §11.3 (covered-by: ui/src/status/descriptors.test.ts completeness sweep) · §11.4 (covered-by: ui/src/{connection,recovery,safety} tests) · §11.5 (covered-by: Phase-8 unpark — Gateway modal/Brain drawer not yet built) · §11.6 (covered-by: ui/src/a11y/reachability tests) · §11.7 (covered-by: ui/src/status + views component tests) · §4 (covered-by: ui/src/gateway-client/mock.ts boundary tests) · §5.1 (covered-by: ui/src/contracts/generated.test.ts) · §7.2 (covered-by: ui/src/connection tests) · §14 (covered-by: the ui Vitest merge-gate suite).

**Track:** `ui` · **Depends on (phases):** 0 (0.5 ✅). _(✅ DONE-modulo-parked 2026-06-08; the parked tail — 6.3d/6.3e + the mutation/intent seam — **Depends on (phases): 2** (mutation-method freeze) **+ 3.4** (Terminal Channel) and resumes the ui track at that freeze.)_

### 6.5 — Graphite Arc theme/visual layer + automated visual gate — ✅ DONE (LEAD-ACCEPTED visual gate 2026-06-08; 6.5a `c439379` + 6.5b `b5618af` + 6.5c `3b6135d`; final aesthetic sign-off flagged for the user)
> **Decomposition (orchestrator, 2026-06-08 — from the 6.5 recon):** 3 visual layers (**non-`/tdd`** — design/visual-gate slices with an optional lightweight wiring-guard; the kit `styles.css` tokens are ALREADY imported in `main.tsx` — the gap is APPLYING them) + the gate, driven layer→layer (Lesson §7). Reference the kit **SEMANTIC** token layer only (never primitives — §11.1 re-hue promise). Target = the assembled prototype `NexusOps-ui-kit/ui_kits/control-plane/index.html` (dark cockpit, Geist, a 3-area CSS Grid, token chrome).
> - **6.5a — base/global layer:** `ui/src/theme/global.css` — `html,body` reset (`--surface-window` bg + Geist `--font-sans` + `--text-primary` + overflow), scrollbar chrome, a global `.sr-only` utility (replaces TopBar's inline `SR_ONLY`). Imported in `main.tsx` after the kit `styles.css`. Wiring-guard: assert the stylesheet is imported + applies `body` styling.
> - **6.5b — shell chrome + grid:** `ui/src/theme/shell.css` — refactor `Shell.tsx` flexbox→the prototype's CSS Grid (`.shell` 3-area: top / sidebar+main / dock); `.topbar`/`.sidebar`/`.main`/`.status-bar`/`.safety-host` token surfaces+borders+elevation; lift `Shell/TopBar/Sidebar/StatusBar` inline structural styles → token classes.
> - **6.5c — view/component panels:** `ui/src/theme/components.css` — `.command-center`/`.sessions`/`.project-graph`/Settings/Usage + the banner/safety surfaces; lift remaining view inline styles → classes (semantic surfaces + type scale + `--sp-*` spacing).
> - **Visual gate (acceptance):** gstack-browser comparison — running dev server vs the prototype — verify dark-cockpit/Geist/grid/chrome MATCH (**human-judged** per Lesson §10). ~700–900 lines CSS + TSX refactor total.
>
> **Why this exists (FINDING, 2026-06-07):** 6.1–6.4 shipped functionally-correct but **completely unstyled** — the kit `styles.css` only DEFINES tokens (`:root` vars); nothing in the app APPLIES them (no `body{}` bg/font, no layout grid, no panel chrome). Only the kit's self-theming components (StatusPill/AttentionMarker) render themed. The Graphite Arc visual layer was never authored, and **no rendered-product verification existed** (jsdom/tsc/oxlint/build don't check appearance — see `ui/LESSONS.md §10`). Human-sequenced as a **dedicated pass at the END of Phase-6 logic** (NOT next); unstyled surfaces are accepted to accumulate until then.

Semantic tokens only (§11.1). _(gate caught a `*/`-in-CSS-comment bug — Lesson §12.)_

## Decisions tabled

Open scope/design questions awaiting resolution, with rationale. **Resolved entries move into the Log (with the resolution) and out of here** — this holds only *open* questions.

- **[RESOLVED 2026-06-13 — edges-R1 cross-track, LEAD-decided (away-authority); flagged for user return-review] The 3 daemon-owned executor-seam design choices + the 2 R1-B contract-locks.** The edges track's R1 ask (the per-namespace executor-registration seam + the P5/P7 wiring event types) folded into the P4 executor-seam work (the seam is also the daemon's own `SessionExecutor` dispatch home). **The 3 design choices (daemon-owned per the consumer-driven proposal):** (1) **async = keep the FROZEN sync `ActionExecutor` trait + `block_on` on the dedicated std::thread** — don't reopen the 2.3/LESSON-20 freeze for a P7.1-only benefit (github/linear hold a `Handle` when they register); **no trait change.** (2) **`ProjectRescanned` = ONE coarse event** (a rescan is atomic; the projector splits projects/repositories rows; smaller contract surface). (3) **registration = incremental `register()` mutation** (a namespace goes live exactly when its handler registers). **The 2 R1-B contract-locks:** (A) **`pr_number: u64`** (a non-negative EXTERNAL natural edges parses from untrusted API responses — positivity structural at the parse boundary; the `seq: i64` internal-counter precedent does NOT apply; sets the precedent for external GitHub naturals). (B) **KEEP `provider` on `GithubSyncFailed`/`LinearSyncFailed`** (§17 mandates distinct per-provider event types → collapse-to-one is off the table; given two types `provider` is redundant-but-spec'd — the edges projector reads it uniformly; benign; edges P7.1 adds a `new()` guard). **Seam-delta findings (vs the stale edges base `a40ac00`):** the registration seam was NOT delivered + the daemon's own 4.0b-1 `SessionExecutor` had no production dispatch home (the seam serves both); `FakeHarness` is NOT yet test-only (the `PtyLauncher` placeholder uses it → the `test-support` feature lands mechanism-only, FakeHarness-gating defers to 4.0b-2); the `*SyncFailed` `auth_expired` 0.5b gate is now LIFTED (frozen at 4.0b-1) but the variant stays deferred (edges' split-choice + a §17/INV-SEC re-review). **No safety-invariant change** (the INV-SEC-1 Adjudication guard PRESERVED through the seam refactor; security PASS every touching layer). `(origin: 2026-06-13 edges-R1 / 4.0b-R1a/R1b)`

- **[RESOLVED 2026-06-12 — P4 forks, USER-decided (present); the B2-strict item flagged for next `/arch-finalize` as a §8 EXTENSION] The 4 P4 live-drive-loop/survival forks.** Ruled at P4 kickoff (deep-dive `docs/planning/P4-deep-dive-live-drive-loop-and-survival.md` §8). **(a) concurrency = opt-3** (session-as-supervised-actor — the daemon's own write-actor idiom [LESSON 9] applied to sessions; sets the `HarnessAdapter` async signature + the supervisor shape every P4 slice lives in). **(b) survival = B2-STRICT** (true live-process reattach — the agent outlives the daemon + reconnects to the live in-flight turn) → **a user-ruled ARCHITECTURE EXTENSION:** §8-as-written specifies `--resume`/`thread/resume` (relaunch-and-resume-from-transcript); B2-strict requires a NEW **detachable-terminal broker subsystem** (a daemon-child PTY agent is SIGHUP-killed when the daemon dies — the deep-dive §7.1 Finding; same for Codex's stdio app-server), and the live survival is **0.1/0.3-HITL-verify-only** (non-deterministic — the deterministic resume/replay/reattach DECISION logic stays test-first). User ruled it IN eyes-open (broker cost + HITL-only verification + the §8 excess). → **§8 to be EXTENDED** with the broker survival subsystem (forward-note in the Phase-4 Spec-anchors + deep-dive §8.1; full prose firms with the 4.1 design) + **flagged for the next `/arch-finalize`** ("don't silently expand — a real arch-contract change"). **(c) ResumeResult** = freeze `ResumeMode = resumed|replayed|relaunched|reattached_live` (the 4th per B2-strict) + `RecoveryState` = the ui's existing 3, at the head of 4.1 (§2.5-seam; the ui has waited since P6) — exact set routed to the user to confirm. **(d) the cat-1 live INV-SEC-1 security pass:** d.1 approval-wait = a configurable §6.2 policy knob (default ~5 min; **fail-closed on timeout/cancel/session-death LOCKED** — not a knob); d.2 split tool-policy (`TodoWrite`+benign-internal auto-allow; `WebFetch`/`WebSearch` require-approval — exfil ≠ benign); d.3 MCP/Task/bg-subagent denied = confirmed MVP. The live-wiring slice (**4.0b**) gets its OWN security-reviewer pass; its cat-1 design surfaces to the lead→user BEFORE Step-2.5 (as 043). `(origin: 2026-06-12 P4 kickoff)`

- **[RESOLVED 2026-06-12 — P4.0b cat-1, USER-intent (present) → away-authority refinement to risk-0; ⚠️ flag for next `/arch-finalize`] session.create/session.kill = RISK-0; 4.0b SPLIT with a binding no-un-intercepted-window condition.** **(a) risk class = RISK-0** (audited auto-allow). The user ruled "risk-1, NOT approval-gated"; the orchestrator surfaced that risk-1 REQUIRES approval (LESSON 19 / `CatalogPolicy`) so risk-1+not-approval-gated is contradictory — the faithful vehicle for the user's intent (routine start, audited, no per-launch approval) is **risk-0** (away-ruled, identical safety behavior, clean over a carve-out; the `workflow.command.invoke`=risk-4 precedent of "make the floor fall out of the model, no carve-out"). **5 PROTECTIVE PINS (required in 4.0b-1 brief/tests):** (a) session.create still emits `SessionStarted` audit → INV-SEC-1 preserved (passes policy + audits, never reaches-around); (b) §15 #8 profile **recorded-at-start**; (c) **session-profile-CHANGE = approval-gated** — the §15 #8 no-silent-account-hop gate lives HERE, not on the routine start; (d) the risk-0-description relaxation is **NARROW to the supervised-session-lifecycle (create/kill)** — NOT a general "mutations may be risk-0"; the no-non-zero-risk-auto-execute safety pin (LESSON 19) stays intact; (e) session.create is **UI/IPC-initiated only** — no agent/Brain path reaches it at risk-0 (agent session-spawns stay denied via #27203/Task-deny; the 043 `AgentMutationPolicy` governs any agent path). To record in the §6.3 catalog rows + Appendix A + §15 note at the 4.0b-1 round. **(b) split:** 4.0b → **4.0b-1** (session.create/kill executor + §15 #8 binding + the 0.5b ExecutionProfile `shared/` freeze [9 values — the §5.1 8 + `credit_exhausted`]) + **4.0b-2** (live INV-SEC-1 interception + the reachable session.create + the real launch, atomic). **🔴 BINDING (non-negotiable, CONFIRMED MET):** no shipped/reachable state runs a real agent un-intercepted → **the guarantee = 4.0b-1 ships NO production caller for session.create + NO real-Claude launch** (the 043 "mechanism built, no live caller" pattern — guaranteed by construction, stronger than fail-closed-paralyzed); 4.0b-2 wires the reachable session.create + the real launch + the interception TOGETHER. Both cat-1; both Step-2.5 surface to the lead. `(origin: 2026-06-12 P4.0b; away-authority risk-0 refinement + 5 pins)`

- **[SEEDED 2026-06-11 — production-posture re-derivation] Full schema-snapshot coverage (all Appendix-A models).** The §2.5-seam rule mandates a schema-snapshot test (field-name set == checked-in snapshot, `spec(§X)`-tagged) only for models whose `§` crosses a §2.5 dependency edge, authored in the same `/tdd` cycle that touches the model. Whether to extend snapshot coverage to ALL Appendix-A models is deliberately tabled — revisit off accumulated `/phase-exit` arch-drift-audit verdicts (if the auditor keeps finding drift on un-snapshotted models, widen; if green, the seam-only rule suffices). `(origin: 2026-06-11 tasks-gen re-derivation)`

- **[RESOLVED 2026-06-11 — Phase-2.1a/2.1b, LEAD-decided (away-authority); flagged for user return-review + next `/arch-finalize`] The 5 Gateway-startup rulings.** Each surfaced BEFORE the freeze/exposure could ship wrong (the §2.5 freeze-early discipline); none required a wire-breaking change. (1) **Gateway IDs = Option A** — `ApprovalId`(`appr_`)/`ActionPlanId`(`aplan_`) are platform-minted prefixed-ULID newtypes OUTSIDE the frozen-22 `IdKind`, off a new `GatewayObjectKind` (sibling to `DesktopObjectKind`); `IdKind` (the PBI cross-product 22) + `test_all_22` untouched. Precedent: the §5.3 desktop objects already live outside `IdKind`. (2) **Status-enum `$def` rename = Option B** — the schemars `$defs` of the frozen `ActionRequest`/`Approval` STATUS enums renamed to their pre-existing Rust aliases `ActionRequestStatus`/`ApprovalStatus`, freeing the bare names for the §6.2 MODELS (schemars keys `$defs` by bare type-name → collision); value-sets unchanged → a `prj_→eprj_`-class value-preserving reconcile (rides the 0.15.0 bump). (3) **`ActionRequest::Denied` → terminal = Option A** — added to `is_terminal()` (the 0.5 freeze omitted it; terminal-by-nature, matches the gateway guard's sink + the Approval machine); Rust-internal, no wire/CONTRACT change. (4) **§15 registry-row redaction = Option A** — `inputs_json` + `resource_refs_json` pass the §15 Redactor before the `action_requests` INSERT (rule #3/#4 "secrets never in ROWS; same redactor gates persist"; mechanism not waiver — untrusted proposers). (5) the orch applied #4's principle to `resource_refs_json` (consistent rule-#4 application) + made the orch-scoped 2.1a/2.1b/2.1c split + the L2/L3 IPC-resequencing (sequencing, lead-endorsed). 2.3-deferred nuance: the real executor reads in-memory validated inputs (not the redacted row) — reconcile §7.2 wording at 2.3. `(origin: 2026-06-11 P2.1a/P2.1b)`
- **[RESOLVED 2026-06-11 — Phase-2.2 L1 §6.3 catalog freeze, LEAD-decided (away-authority); flag for user return-review + next `/arch-finalize`] `workflow.command.invoke` = risk-4 (critical) in the ActionTypeCatalog.** The §6.3 MVP catalog's locked risk for arbitrary pack-command execution (shell/hook packs, §13.1). Ruled **risk-4** (not the brief's risk-3 default), grounded in the LOCKED §6.3: "`workflow.command.invoke` with null `input_schema` is risk-floored to require approval — **cannot be standing-granted** (OQ-WP-5)." The classification that STRUCTURALLY enforces "never standing-granted / never approve-all" IS risk-4 (§6.2 + the §11.5 Review-Modal "critical/4 excluded from approve-all"). Risk-3 is approve-all-eligible/standing-grantable → classifying it risk-3 CONTRADICTS the §6.3 floor + forces an ugly "risk-3-but-not-standing-grantable" carve-out; risk-4 makes the floor fall out of the risk model (no carve-out). Blast radius UNBOUNDED (arbitrary command + untyped null-schema inputs) → critical by definition, strictly worse than the bounded `git.delete_worktree` (3-4). Bonus: gives the MVP catalog a REAL risk-4 entry so the critical-exclusion (#10) + no-non-zero-auto-execute (#9) safety pins test against a production type, not a synthetic injected catalog. **Forward:** when the typed-`input_schema` case is built, the §6.2 `PolicyDecision.downgrade` path can contextually lower scrutiny for a *bounded typed* invocation — but the LOCKED catalog risk stays 4 (conservative floor); a `downgrade`-path forward consideration, NOT a catalog change. This is the §6.3 per-action resolution the catalog freeze pins (not a locked-text change). To record in the ActionTypeCatalog Appendix-A row at `/orchestrate-end`. `(origin: 2026-06-11 P2.2 L1)`
- **[RESOLVED 2026-06-11 — Phase-2.3 L1 §15 safety-design, LEAD-decided (away-authority); flagged for user return-review + next `/arch-finalize`] Idempotency-key derivation = Option A (one-way SHA-256 of RAW inputs).** A NEW §15 precedent surfaced by the impl at 2.3-L1 Step-2.5: the dedup fingerprint persists in the indexed `action_requests.idempotency_key` column and **must** derive from RAW (unredacted) inputs — hashing the §15-redacted inputs would make two actions differing only in a secret hash identically → the 2nd silently deduped away (a correctness + availability bug). So this column **by-design bypasses the §15 `inputs_json` row-redactor** (the 2.1a rule-#4 mechanism). Ruled **Option A** — derive `idempotency_key` as a **one-way SHA-256 of the raw inputs** (`FromInputs`: hash `action_type\0project_id\0canonical_json(inputs)`; `NaturalResourceRef`: hash `action_type\0sorted(resource_ref ids)`; `None`: no key), persist the hash; the requester-supplied key is IGNORED (recorded-not-trusted — pinned by `idem_key_overrides_requester_supplied`, closing a proposer-chosen-key collision-to-suppress attack). **Rule-#4-compliant:** a one-way hash is a FINGERPRINT, not the secret; correct usage keeps real secrets in the keychain (`keychain_ref`s in `inputs`, never raw secrets) → the hash reveals nothing recoverable — **the keychain_ref convention is what makes A safe.** Chosen over **C (HMAC-SHA256 w/ a keychain daemon key)** because A gives **stable, deterministic-forever** dedup (the feature's purpose — prevent duplicate mutations on retry/crash-recovery), whereas a daemon-key reset would break HMAC fingerprints → dedup misses → **duplicate execution** = a safety regression for a mutation-dedup feature; "heavy ≠ correct." **B (hash redacted inputs)** rejected (false dedup → silent victim-action suppression). **Bounded residual:** a low-entropy secret WRONGLY placed inline (rule-#4 misuse) could be brute-forced from its hash — bounded by (misuse + low entropy + local-DB read = already past the trust boundary, rule #7). **C = the documented HARDENING; upgrade trigger:** a real low-entropy-inline-secret threat surfaces, OR `idempotency_key` data ever **leaves the local trust boundary** (off-machine backup/sync) — at which point "the DB holds no recoverable secrets" becomes load-bearing against an off-machine attacker. Adds the `sha2` (RustCrypto) dep (cargo audit at `/phase-exit 2`). To record in the §15 ARCHITECTURE.md note + LESSONS at L1 Step-9/round. `(origin: 2026-06-11 P2.3 L1)`
- **[RESOLVED 2026-06-11 — Phase-2.4 L1 §6.4 contract gap, LEAD-decided (away-authority); flagged for user return-review + next `/arch-finalize`] §6.4 error-code set extension = Option A (add `internal_error` + `fencing_conflict`).** A LOCKED-§6.4 edit surfaced by the impl at 2.4-L1: the new §17 failures (`AuditWriteFailed`/`FencingConflict`/`StalePrecondition`/`UnknownOutcome`) need honest IPC codes; the LOCKED 7-code set (`version_skew, frame_too_large, unknown_method, unauthorized_peer, policy_denied, precondition_stale, protocol_error`) has none for fencing/internal. Ruled **Option A** — add **`internal_error`** + **`fencing_conflict`** (frozen in L1 with the event taxonomy at CONTRACT 0.19.0 + the IpcErrorCode snapshot). **Architecturally DETERMINED (not a taste call):** the codes drive THREE distinct §11.5 safety cards with DIFFERENT resolution semantics — `fencing_conflict`→the **never-auto-resolved** hard-conflict card (**rule #6**) · `precondition_stale`→the **re-approvable** stale card · `internal_error`→the **fail-closed** integrity alert (`AuditWriteFailed`+`UnknownOutcome`; the L1 structured `ActionFailed.ActionError` detail carries the finer distinction). **B rejected** (collapsing all→`precondition_stale` is SAFETY-wrong: the UI couldn't tell a never-auto-resolved fencing conflict from a re-approvable stale card → renders fencing as re-approvable → **breaks rule #6 at the UI layer**); **C rejected** (fencing still conflated with stale — the most load-bearing distinction). +2 wire codes, **ADDITIVE** (the ui tolerant-reads + regenerates; adopts them at its resume when it builds the §11.5 cards), rides the 0.19.0 bump, resolves the 2.1b `internal_error` carry-forward. Mechanism precedent = the `protocol_error` §6.4 extension. Mapping (L2-L5 behavior): `AuditWriteFailed`/`UnknownOutcome`→`internal_error` · `FencingConflict`→`fencing_conflict` · `StalePrecondition`→`precondition_stale`. To record in the §6.4 + Appendix-A + `daemon/CLAUDE.md` cross-doc edits at the 2.4 round seal. `(origin: 2026-06-11 P2.4 L1)`
- **[RESOLVED 2026-06-07 — §15 safety-design, human-decided] 1.1 redaction sequencing = Option (a+).** 1.1 ships the **`redaction_status` column + `Redactor` trait + fail-closed gate** (never persist `redaction_status='unredacted'`) + the **high-recall token-prefix Redactor**; the **entropy fallback (OQ-SEC-2) is a BLOCKING Phase-1 task (1.7)**, not a fast-follow — chosen so secret-detection recall can't drift. Pin the §15 invariant with the fail-closed test (the load-bearing assertion). `redaction_status` on the envelope = cross-doc invariant → Appendix A / §7.1 / DATA_MODEL §2.1 atomic edit at 1.1 Step 9. `(origin: 2026-06-07 P1.1)`

- **[RESOLVED 2026-06-08 — §17 / safety-rule-#6, human-decided] Lease fencing authority = Option B (a LIVE lease).** §17 (line 387) pinned "stale-token write → `fencing_conflict`" but left **"stale" undefined** for the *expired-but-not-yet-superseded* holder (the zombie-holder window) — and §17's daemon-crash recovery (line 384) re-derives an old `executing` action via the same "lease check." Human ruled **Option B**: authority = a **live lease** — `locks::validate_held` = owner-match AND `token == fencing_token` AND `expires_at > now`; **"stale" = expired OR superseded**. Rejected Option A (textbook supersession-only — leaves the zombie window) + Option C (defer to Phase 2 — would ship the safety semantic untested). Pinned by the 1.4 L1 test `test_expired_holder_rejected_even_if_unsuperseded`. Recorded: §17 lines 387/384 clarified + Appendix A `Lease` row + `daemon/CLAUDE.md` cross-doc row + LESSONS §6. **Phase-2 dependency (task 2.4):** the gateway must heartbeat/renew (or re-acquire) a long-running action so it doesn't self-fence on its own lease expiry. `(origin: 2026-06-08 P1.4)`
- **[RESOLVED 2026-06-08 — §6.4 contract gap, LEAD-decided (away-authority)] Add `protocol_error` to the §6.4 error-code set (Option B).** P1.5 L2 surfaced that the LOCKED §6.4 6-code set (`version_skew, frame_too_large, unknown_method, unauthorized_peer, policy_denied, precondition_stale`) has **no code for a bad-first-frame / handshake-required / protocol violation** (a non-Hello or malformed first frame). L2 shipped `unknown_method` as a safe, test-pinned placeholder; the lead ruled **Option B** (add `protocol_error`) on the user's behalf — production-grade / correct-the-first-time (the IPC contract is new at 0.9.0 + **UNCONSUMED** by the ui, so correcting before consumption is ~free). Rejected Option A (ratify `unknown_method` — imprecise; the ui + future RemoteClient couldn't distinguish a protocol violation from an unknown method). **Execute within the 1.5 round (before seal):** impl folds `protocol_error` into `IpcErrorCode` + swaps the bad-first-frame emission + re-pins test 7 + bumps **CONTRACT_VERSION 0.9.0→0.10.0** + regen + 3-way verify; orchestrator writes the §6.4 + Appendix-A + cross-doc edits hot at `/orchestrate-end`. **Away-authority transparency:** in the lead's away-log + here for the user's review; re-validated at the next user-invoked `/arch-finalize`. `(origin: 2026-06-08 P1.5 L2 Finding)`
- **[RESOLVED 2026-06-12 — P3.2-part-2/043 cat-1 INV-SEC-1 interception, LEAD-decided (away-authority, 3 human safety gates); flagged for user return-review + next `/arch-finalize`] The Claude `MutationIntercept`→Gateway interception design.** The first real harness mutation chokepoint (a `PreToolUse` hook → daemon receiver → an **adjudication-only `ActionRequest`** through the **EXISTING** Gateway pipeline → audit-before-verdict `MutationVerdict{Allow|Deny}`; the daemon ADJUDICATES + AUDITS, the agent executes). **Rulings:** **(Q1) = Option A** — extend the §6.3 catalog with an agent-mutation family routed through the one pipeline (NOT a parallel path — one chokepoint to prove INV-SEC-1-safe); agent-mutation ActionRequests are **adjudication-only** (terminate at the verdict; `ExecutorKind::Adjudication`, no daemon executor — code-enforced: pipeline-terminal + a `CatalogExecutor::execute` fail-safe + #13 RecordingExecutor-never-called). **(audit-before-verdict, §15 #5)** — an Allow is gated on the authoritative event committed FIRST; audit-write-fault → Deny (proven via `FaultPoint::AuditEventWrite`). **(Q2) = risk-tiered conservative** — read-only (risk-0) auto-allow (read-only ≠ mutation); **every mutating tool requires approval BY DEFAULT** (no auto-allow until a user standing-grant); deny-rules deny known-dangerous; no/failed/timed-out → Deny. **(A1) = record-then-deny** — a blocked dangerous attempt is NEVER silently dropped from the audit log: the agent deny-rule path commits `ActionRequested`+`ActionDenied{None, reason}` (the 2.4 record-then-throw precedent, `is_agent_mutation`-gated; non-agent denials keep the Err-rollback). **The O-13 coverage-gap crux + the CLOSED INV-SEC-1 residual:** a default-mode `PreToolUse` hook-MISS fails OPEN (verified) — three un-interceptable categories (MCP=BestEffort · Task=NotGuaranteed · bg-subagent=#27203). **Compensated + CLOSED** (no surviving residual): only DirectToolUse-default-mode → Adjudicate; MCP/Task/bg/non-default → DENY two-layer (receiver-deny + hook-INDEPENDENT `permissions.deny:["mcp__*","Task"]`); + the **cheap-closure** — generated settings carry NO `permissions.allow` (the hook is the sole allow-authority) + a 5s hook timeout (not 600s), so in a non-interactive PTY the never-populated first-use cache → a hook-miss has nothing to fall through to → BLOCK = fail-CLOSED. **Contract:** 2 §2.5-seam bumps — 0.21→**0.22** (the §6.3 `AGENT_MUTATION_ACTION_TYPES` [4 `agent.*`, MVP-22 unchanged] + `ExecutorKind::Adjudication`) → **0.23** (the `ActionDenied.approval_id`→`Option<String>` relax — honest: a policy-deny has no approval; daemon-internal/un-consumed/additive-tolerant). **Tradeoff (return-review):** supervised Claude **cannot use MCP tools** in the MVP (the BestEffort-channel deny). 5 layers, security-reviewer PASS every layer (0 crit/0 high; the INV-SEC-1 core passed ADVERSARIAL verification — no Allow-without-audit, no executor-runs-for-agent-mutation). The live drive-loop wiring + the per-session decision_sink = **P4** (Carry-forward). LESSON §26. `(origin: 2026-06-12 P3.2-part-2/043)`
- **[RESOLVED 2026-06-12 — cat-4 / load-bearing, LEAD-decided (away-authority); flagged for user return-review + next `/arch-finalize`] SDK-vs-PTY primary driver for Claude (O-4 / ADR-006) = PTY-PRIMARY (Branch A). The drain is DISSOLVED.** The deciding question (`OQ-HARN-SPIKE-7 §3`: does SDK/`-p` pool exhaustion hard-stop a supervised session with no interactive fallback while interactive stays exempt?) was **resolved from DOCS, no empirical drain** — claude-code-guide VERIFIED the June-15-2026 billing split against the **primary Help-Center source `support.claude.com/articles/15036540`** + corroborating official docs: Agent-SDK/`-p` draws a **separate monthly credit pool** (Pro $20 · Max-5× $100 · Max-20× $200) that **HARD-STOPS with no interactive fallback** on exhaustion (unless API-rate overflow is manually enabled); interactive Claude Code runs on **auto-resetting rolling-window limits** (5h+7d); **no real-time SDK-credit-balance is exposed** (no SDK/statusLine field, no API — GH #47574; statusLine DOES expose `rate_limits.{five_hour,seven_day}.used_percentage` + context-% + cost). So the §0.1 drain (real-usage exhaustion measurement) was never needed for the meter DATA (already deterministic + frozen 3.1) — it targeted only the exhaustion-asymmetry, which is **docs-determinable = YES**. **RULING = Branch A, PTY-PRIMARY:** a control plane must not brick a supervised run on a hard-stopping capped pool with no fallback (Branch B's confirmed failure mode); Branch A rides the self-recovering subscription rolling-window. Branch A's coarser hook-based interception is acceptable because the O-13 mitigations it leans on (default-mode-only + `PreToolUse` deny-rules + no-bg-subagents #27203) are **ALREADY LOCKED regardless of branch** → backstop elevated to primary, **NO new safety surface**; INV-SEC-1 + #9 + #10 intact; **REVERSIBLE** (the 3.1 trait is daemon-internal/unfrozen → SDK-primary stays an additive future option, no contract break). **Applied 3.2-part-1 (042):** `ClaudeAdapter::launch()` = interactive PTY via `PortablePtyHost`, default-mode-only, no `-p`, no bg subagents, hooks+statusLine wired — the spec IS the #10 enforcement surface; SDK = a secondary status/telemetry signal, NOT the drive transport. **Sub-decisions ratified (round 016):** §6.4 terminal encoding = **JSON-base64 MVP** @0.21.0 (binary fast-path additively deferred to 3.5-with-throughput; the `ServerFrame` reserved-slot doc + LESSON §7) · `terminal_id` = a **daemon runtime handle** (`String` wire / typed newtype via the §14 IdGen seam), NOT a 23rd LOCKED-22 `IdKind` (the `subscription_id` precedent). **0.5b:** the ExecutionProfile runtime states `rate_limited` (soft/auto-recovering interactive) + `credit_exhausted` (hard SDK-pool stop) freeze when 3.2 reaches the profile; the remaining 0.5b profile/account-binding stays HITL-parked. **§11.4 meter (banked, downstream):** two pools — interactive rolling-window % (live statusLine) + SDK monthly-credit ESTIMATE (local spend-ledger vs per-tier ceiling, no real-time API). The §0.1/§9.1 Appendix-A + the ADR-006 mode-PENDING-SPIKE marker get the AS-BUILT PTY-primary note at this round + the next `/arch-finalize`. `(origin: 2026-06-07 P0.1; resolved 2026-06-12 P3.2 round)`

Carry as a known Phase-10 concern; resolve before the integration/deploy gate. `(origin: 2026-06-07, D14 audit)`
- **[RESOLVED 2026-06-08 → ✅ REALIZED 1.6c 2026-06-10 (`aeedc3f` L1 + `4df4078` L2) — §17/§15 audit-integrity safety call, USER-RULED Option C] Corrupt-event-row recovery on `open` (the deferred 1.2 L3 Finding). Realized in 1.6c (quarantine + degrade-not-crash + `AuditIntegrityViolation` event; LESSON §11; §17 row 401 marked [IMPLEMENTED 1.6c]).** **USER RULING: Option C** — on a corrupt event row in `catch_up_replay`: quarantine (preserve) the raw row + emit a loud audit-integrity event + mark the offset degraded + continue (daemon starts); never *silently* drop; raw spine untouched (§17 as written). Adds the audit-integrity event type → CONTRACT_VERSION bump in 1.6c; security-reviewer applies. 1.2's `catch_up_replay` rebuilds projections via the *strict* read path, so one corrupt event row aborts `open()` → daemon won't start. Audit-integrity dimension: a naive skip could *silently* drop a real event. **What §17 already says (locked):** eventstore row — *corrupt `payload_json` → quarantine + audit-integrity event*; *unknown version → store raw + degraded marker, don't crash*; projections row — *mark offset degraded, skip bad event, never corrupt raw events.* Options:
  - **A — silent skip-and-continue:** ✗ violates audit-integrity (projection silently diverges from true history, no record). Reject.
  - **B — refuse-to-start + restore `.bak`:** ✓ integrity, ✗ availability (one corrupt row → daemon won't start), ✗ may lose more events than it saves (stale pre-migration `.bak`). This is §16's *migration-failure* semantics mis-applied to a corrupt event row.
  - **C — quarantine + audit-integrity event + continue (= §17 as written). ⭐ ORCHESTRATOR RECOMMENDS.** Quarantine (preserve) the corrupt raw row, emit a loud audit-integrity event recording the gap, mark the offset degraded, continue replay, daemon starts. No *silent* drop · daemon starts · raw spine untouched. **Rationale:** for an audited single-mutator system the only unacceptable failure is *silent* divergence — C makes the gap loud + recorded + recoverable; it is **conformance to locked §17, not a fresh architectural call** (surfaced because it is audit-integrity-sensitive). Cost: adds an audit-integrity event type → CONTRACT_VERSION bump in the 1.6c sub-slice. `(origin: 2026-06-08 P1.2 L3 Finding; lead-parked-for-user 2026-06-08)`
- **[RESOLVED 2026-06-08 — INV-SEC-1/§15 single-mutator safety call, USER-RULED Option B] Cold-start Device/LocalRunner registration mechanism. Unblocks 1.6a-L3 (the registration layer).** **USER RULING: Option B** — direct system-event append via the write-actor (`actor_type=System`), through the §15 redaction gate + projector fold (immutable + audited), NOT policy-gated. **Both sub-leans ADOPTED:** (i) reserved system-workspace sentinel + (ii) `DeviceId`/`LocalRunnerId` `minted_id!` newtypes off `DesktopObjectKind` (NOT new `IdKind` variants). Adds `DeviceRegistered`/`LocalRunnerRegistered` → CONTRACT_VERSION bump in 1.6a-L3; security-reviewer applies (INV-SEC-1-touching). §16 puts desktop-host `Device` + per-start `LocalRunner` registration at bootstrap step (2) — **before the UI connects and before the Action Gateway exists (Phase 2).** INV-SEC-1: "*no code path mutates … session state except via a typed Action that passed policy+approval.*" Options:
  - **A — Gateway Action:** ✗ circular (Gateway is Phase 2) → forces registration to defer to P2, blocking the §16 cold-start ordering. Reject for P1.
  - **B — direct system-event append via the write-actor (`actor_type=System`). ⭐ ORCHESTRATOR RECOMMENDS.** Bootstrap self-registration is the daemon establishing its *own* runtime identity — substrate (like the event log recording system facts / `SessionStarted` in 1.2), not an intent from an untrusted proposer (agent/Brain/UI) mutating external state. It still produces an immutable, **redaction-gated, audited** event (the §15 append gate + projector fold both run); it is simply not a *policy-gated Action* because there is no untrusted intent to gate. **INV-SEC-1 governs proposer-driven mutations; the daemon's own lifecycle events are the layer those Actions are recorded *into*.** Cost: adds `LocalRunnerRegistered` + `DeviceRegistered` event types → the CONTRACT_VERSION bump 1.6a-L3 already carries.
  - **Two contract-shaping sub-decisions that apply IF Option B (surfaced at 1.6a Step-2.5; settle with the mechanism call):** (i) **`workspace_id` on bootstrap events** — the envelope's `workspace_id` is NOT NULL, but cold-start runs before any workspace. Orch lean: a **reserved system-workspace sentinel** (a well-known `ws_` constant for System-actor lifecycle events — the "system scope" pattern), NOT minting a host-workspace (conflates Workspace=project-container with the daemon host) and NOT a nullable-column schema change to the frozen envelope. Sets the workspace-less-event precedent. (ii) **`lr_`/`dev_` id newtypes** — no `DeviceId`/`LocalRunnerId` newtype exists (the 22 `IdKind` = 16 minted + 6 external; the 4 desktop prefixes live only on `DesktopObjectKind::id_prefix()`). dev_/lr_ are **consumer-facing** (the UI reads LocalRunner). Orch lean: add `DeviceId`/`LocalRunnerId` `minted_id!` newtypes in `shared/` off the frozen `DesktopObjectKind` prefixes (keyed via `DesktopObjectKind`, NOT new `IdKind` variants — don't touch the frozen-22), rather than the bare-String `out_` precedent (which is for genuinely-internal ids). (Also an L3 build wrinkle, non-user: object_refs must source the lr_/dev_ id from the payload since it isn't an envelope typed column.) `(origin: 2026-06-08 P1.6a-L3; lead-parked-for-user 2026-06-08)`
- **[RESOLVED 2026-06-10 — §5.3/§16 frozen-contract contradiction, USER-RULED Option A] Device-role = MVP-live desktop-host.** 1.6a-L3 (registering a desktop-host Device for the first time) surfaced a pre-existing architecture self-contradiction: the **binding** §16 first-run-bootstrap step (`ARCHITECTURE.md:376`) mandates the daemon "registers desktop-host `Device`+`LocalRunner`" (→ Device live at every cold-start), while the frozen §5.3 (`:161`) + Appendix-A (`:513`) + `DesktopObjectKind::Device.is_deferred()` + the frozen test `test_desktop_objects_defined_and_deferred_marked` all classified Device as "dormant iOS scaffolding [DEFERRED]." L3 correctly implemented the binding §16, exposing the §5.3 "deferred" marker as the stale side. **NOT a re-open of registration=Option-B** (the mechanism ruling stands). Escalated as a contract-surface decision (the user wants to ratify frozen-row changes). **USER RULING: Option A** — the local desktop-host **Device is MVP-live** (the stable per-host identity; LocalRunner is per-start and cannot be it); `is_deferred()` is now `false` for Device (true only for RemoteClient); the iOS multi-device/pairing dimension of Device + RemoteClient stay deferred (captured as §5.3 prose, not the predicate). Rejected Option B (keep `is_deferred=true` + a note — leaves the predicate self-contradictory: registers an object the contract still labels dormant). Option C ("don't register a Device") was off the table — the binding §16 mandates it. Recorded: §5.3 + §16 + Appendix-A (objects + EventTypeRegistry rows) + `daemon/CLAUDE.md` cross-doc + LESSON §10; code reconcile (`is_deferred()` + the frozen test) folded into the L3 commit `980e1a0`. **Contract-surface + locked-§5.3 edit on the user's ruling → re-validate at the next user-invoked `/arch-finalize`.** `(origin: 2026-06-10 P1.6a-L3 Step-9 Finding)`
- **[RESOLVED 2026-06-11 — §15 redactor-recall safety-design, USER-RULED Option B] Redactor extend-detection beyond `KEY=value`.** 2.0-SEC L1 measured the `prefix-entropy-v2` envelope against a synthetic labeled corpus (11 secrets / 11 non-secrets): **recall_catchable 1.0 / precision 1.0 / FP-rate 0.0**; per-category recall Prefixed 1.0 · KvValue 0.75 · JsonValue 0.5 · BareRun 0.5 (overall 0.727) — quantifying the three §15 line-360 residuals: **(a)** short JSON-`"key":"value"` non-prefixed secrets, **(b)** hex≈git-SHA (~4.0 bits — **irreducible by tuning**: lowering the bar to catch hex secrets also masks real git-SHAs → FP↑), **(c)** adversarial split-into-<20-char. Escalated with the measured report + scored options. **USER RULING: Option B** — extend **JSON-value detection with an ID-allowlist FP guard, mask-in-place**, preserving the measured 0% FP-rate; closes residual (a) — the gap that grows exactly as **2.1 widens the JSON action-payload capture surface**. **Option C (sensitivity-gated quarantine-bias) DEFERRED** — LESSON §13's mask-in-place-over-divert preference holds; a fast-follow only if real sensitive-payload traffic later warrants arming the MVP-unreached divert. Residuals **(b)+(c) stay accepted-and-owned** ((b) irreducible; (c) adversarial — primary control is rule #5 keychain-refs-only). Rejected **A** (accept-as-measured — leaves the growing (a) gap unaddressed at the moment 2.1 widens the surface). Realized in **2.0-SEC L3** (new JSON-value pass at the KV-confidence bar + ID-allowlist; **`ENGINE_VERSION` bumps** — recall bar moved; free-string value → **no CONTRACT_VERSION bump**; security-reviewer applies). §15 line-360 + LESSON §13 refined with the measured+extended envelope at L3 Step 9. `(origin: 2026-06-11 P2.0-SEC L1 measurement → human ruling)`
- **[RESOLVED 2026-06-11 — §18 perf-budget re-baseline, USER-RULED Option A — settled, NOT flagged-for-review] §18 write-path CI guards re-baselined to the AS-BUILT Gateway submit (realistic-load model).** The 2.5 §18 benchmark (built against the real `submit_action`→CatalogPolicy→CatalogExecutor→write-actor path) surfaced that the committed §14 CI guards (write p95 < 30 ms / p99 < 75 ms / ≥ 1,500 commits/s) were the **0.4 raw-append proxy** (a single bare INSERT, 5.35 ms) — a path that **does not exist in production**: every commit carries the mandatory §15 redactor-v3 scan + in-band projections (incl. the audit-projector FTS) + outbox + object_refs, and a risk-0 submit = **3 such commits × ~8–12 statements**. Measured AS-BUILT: realistic (non-saturating) submit p95 **~6 ms** (p50 ~2.5 ms), sustained **~1,378 events/s** capacity; the saturating N=20 zero-think-time burst hits ~122 ms / ~844 ev/s (pathological worst case, NOT a realistic-use bound). Escalated as a cat-2 Finding with options. **USER RULING: Option A** — re-baseline the §14 CI guards to the AS-BUILT submit under a **realistic non-saturating** load model: **p95 < 25 ms** (≈4× over the ~6 ms baseline; well under the 100 ms user SLO) + sustained **≥ 1,000 events/s**; **retire** the raw-append 30/75 ms / 1,500; the p99 + the saturating-stress ceiling + the N=100/@1M sweep become **reported-not-gated**. Add an as-built §18 row distinguishing raw-append (proxy) vs full Gateway submit. Throughput gated on **events-committed/s** (== commits/s — the §18-basis unit), not submits/s (≈ events/s ÷ 3). Rejected **B** (saturating-stress re-baseline → a guard *looser* than the 100 ms SLO) + **C** (keep the raw numbers → gate permanently red against a path that doesn't exist). **Recorded:** `ARCHITECTURE.md §18` (budget rows + the §14 guard table + the as-built note) — this round's orch hot-write; the 2.5 bench asserts the matching `WRITE_P95_MAX=25ms` / `THROUGHPUT_FLOOR=1000 ev/s` constants. **Secondary finding** (Carry-forward, future perf-hardening): the audit-projector FTS DELETE+INSERT-per-event O(n) cost (throughput halved 15k→30k under saturation); Phase-10 lever **(D)** out-of-band projections / background checkpoint. **Directly user-ruled (not away-authority) — settled.** `(origin: 2026-06-11 P2.5 §18 benchmark Finding → user ruling)`
- _(Phase 0 also populated §18 perf numbers (0.4 → written into `ARCHITECTURE.md §18`). §13.1 Brain transport still resolves later.)_
